the local noun selects alterity and exception, with affective boundary pressure but not the verbal change sense","qac_refs":["1:7:5:1"],"root":{"arabic":"غ ي ر","transliteration":"gh-y-r"},"surface":{"arabic":"غَيْرِ","transliteration":"ghayri"}},{"analysis_record_ref":"1:7:6","analytic_gloss_range_en":"definite passive participle naming the wrath-incurred class, with the affected recipients foregrounded and the wrath agent left unspoken","analytic_root_gloss_range_en":"wrath, anger, swelling heat, and reactive force fields; the local passive participle selects those upon whom wrath is directed rather than narrating the act or naming the agent","qac_refs":["1:7:6:1","1:7:6:2"],"root":{"arabic":"غ ض ب","transliteration":"gh-ḍ-b"},"surface":{"arabic":"ٱلْمَغْضُوبِ","transliteration":"al-maghḍūbi"}},{"analysis_record_ref":"1:7:7","analytic_gloss_range_en":"second upon-them complement, completing the passive wrath construction by marking the target on whom wrath is directed","analytic_root_gloss_range_en":null,"qac_refs":["1:7:7:1","1:7:7:2"],"root":{"note":"no-root"},"surface":{"arabic":"عَلَيْهِمْ","transliteration":"ʿalayhim"}},{"analysis_record_ref":"1:7:8","analytic_gloss_range_en":"coordinating conjunction that carries the second excluded class under the same restrictive frame rather than starting a new narrative unit","analytic_root_gloss_range_en":null,"qac_refs":["1:7:8:1"],"root":{"note":"no-root"},"surface":{"arabic":"وَ","transliteration":"wa"}},{"analysis_record_ref":"1:7:9","analytic_gloss_range_en":"negative particle that carries the prior exclusion across coordination to the final class and keeps that class outside the requested path","analytic_root_gloss_range_en":null,"qac_refs":["1:7:8:2"],"root":{"note":"no-root"},"surface":{"arabic":"لَا","transliteration":"lā"}},{"analysis_record_ref":"1:7:10","analytic_gloss_range_en":"definite genitive masculine plural active participle naming the astray as the final excluded class, with straying left absolute rather than tied to an explicit complement","analytic_root_gloss_range_en":"straying, losing the way, error, forgetting, disappearance, and loss of trace; 1:7 `ٱلضَّآلِّينَ` (`ض ل ل`)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Wildlife Generation and Exposure","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_08433633adf6b9747ef9","text":"New animal life emerges from reproductive enclosure into an open field populated by birds, herd animals, and predators.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Water-Secured Encampment and Livelihood","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_08bd0289d6623c724586","text":"The well or collected water supplies the resource, control of water makes encampment possible, irrigation extends it into the surrounding land, and livelihood is the resulting stable state.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Compassionate Provision and Nurture","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_0b6a58a3663ce0d84403","text":"Compassion is the motive, progressive nurture and guardianship are the continuing operations, and food, equipment, and assistance are the practical means. Blessing names the resulting condition of the cared-for dependent.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Governed and Pleasant Settlement","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_0dcb4502d9cc867eb0d4","text":"A community establishes residence in a governed place, finds the site suitable, and remains there.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Hunt, Flight, and Scattered Routes","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_0dd39368e528ad394e60","text":"Departure initiates pursuit, flight breaks collective formation, and diverging roads spatialize the resulting separation. Loss names the outcome from the perspective of the group or holder that can no longer retain what fled.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Patronage, Support, and Claimed Recognition","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_0f3f13cb3862e15134ea","text":"Aid, mainstay, and livelihood form one support mechanism. The social relation becomes patronage when the giver converts that material dependence into a demand for acknowledgment.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Sky, Rain, Wind, and Enduring Growth","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_0fa029148c4bbd9de82f","text":"1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); 1:2 `رَبِّ` (`ر ب ب`); 1:5 `نَسْتَعِينُ` (`ع و ن`); 1:7 `أَنْعَمْتَ` and `غَيْرِ` (`ن ع م`, `غ ي ر`)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Consecrated Offering","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_11e9a14d99d75c741614","text":"1:1 `ٱللَّهِ` and 1:2 `لِلَّهِ` (`ء ل ه`); 1:5 `نَعْبُدُ` (`ع ب د`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`); 1:7 `أَنْعَمْتَ` (`ن ع م`)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Hardness, Shielding, and the Cutting Edge","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_127ebc7f36fd8e0b1c4c","text":"The sword supplies penetrating force, the shield supplies a worked resistant surface, rock supplies the material analogy for hardness, and bodily solidity supplies the agent capable of holding the defense.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Thickening, Coating, and Repair","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_15710b2f5f88a9b5b705","text":"Stability is produced by cohesive material, load-bearing members, mechanical parts, and treatments that bind, coat, or repair.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Rivalry, Resistance, and Repeated War","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1964b54bd12df4a61060","text":"1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); 1:2 `ٱلْحَمْدُ` (`ح م د`); 1:5 `نَسْتَعِينُ` (`ع و ن`); 1:6 `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ق و م`); 1:7 `ٱلْمَغْضُوبِ` (`غ ض ب`)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Herd, Livestock, and the Stray Margin","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1a1a0586d3f5122a37f6","text":"Species and age make animals classifiable, gathering makes them a herd, and the lead animal provides internal order. The stray defines the boundary case where an animal loses both group placement and known ownership.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Thickening, Coating, and Repair","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1b98b45df3a162bce650","text":"A thick material is worked to cohesion, applied as a coating or preservative, and used to repair containers, food, medicine, or transport surfaces.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Rivalry, Resistance, and Repeated War","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1cd10680dae7a8d55407","text":"Opposition escalates through rivalry and anger into combat, retaliation, defense, defeat, or compensatory settlement.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Herd, Livestock, and the Stray Margin","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1dc2fc0be37c2dbf7db0","text":"Domestic and wild animals gather into recognizable herds, are classified and led, and can fall outside the group as ownerless strays.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Daylight, Zenith, and Celestial Marking","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1f85dbb11a21596cfbc4","text":"Time is made intelligible by bounded spans and celestial positions, by pace within a duration, and by a culminating event of judgment.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Daylight, Zenith, and Celestial Marking","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_20c3e3db6cae3a8e9884","text":"A daylight span is located by the sun at zenith and by visible signs in the overhead sky.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Daylight, Zenith, and Celestial Marking","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_212666ac1241574c4635","text":"Daylight supplies the bounded interval, zenith gives it an internal midpoint, and sky signs provide a larger celestial coordinate system. Time is read through the positions of bodies that appear to stand overhead.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Lesion, Swelling, and Localized Pain","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_229f93bd2c02ad00f9f7","text":"1:1 and 1:3 `ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ` / `ٱلرَّحِيمِ` (`ر ح م`); 1:2 `ٱلْعَٰلَمِينَ` (`ع ل م`); 1:6 `ٱلْمُسْتَقِيمَ` (`ق و م`); 1:7 `ٱلْمَغْضُوبِ` (`غ ض ب`)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Rivalry, Resistance, and Repeated War","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_265a745f3d2131841709","text":"Competition supplies the initiating comparison, resolve converts comparison into action, resistance supplies reciprocal force, and repeated war stabilizes opposition as an enduring social frame. Praise records the reputation won within that contest.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Anger, Mourning, Vengeance, and Redress","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_29ce299c8c3a7f753636","text":"Injury activates anger, death or irreversible loss introduces grief, and retaliation offers one outcome. Indemnity supplies an alternate endpoint in which compensation replaces vengeance and prevents blood from remaining without redress.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Herd, Livestock, and the Stray Margin","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_2a4334ee226b4873d5f8","text":"Animal life is organized by gathering, classification, reproduction, leadership, pursuit, and scattering.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Well and Water-Lifting Assembly","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_2a6809cf3e58618d4fba","text":"An upright timber and working components are arranged over a water source so a settlement can draw and control water.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Covenant, Refuge, and Legal Protection","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_2dd635b578103278e68f","text":"1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`);
